23:27:33 run E37 20260918-232733: What does seasonal-wildlife cost in FPS, and does that cost grow with the load the fort is already under? Every experiment so far has measured what the tool DOES; none has measured what it charges. DF's own achieved frame rate is readable at `enabler.calculated_fps` and the harness already samples it as `fps_achieved` at every clock probe, so the measure is DF's own number rather than wall-clock timing that would include RPC overhead. The cap is raised to 10,000 in every arm so the cap is never the binding constraint (NEVER zero — that freezes DF permanently; the rig refuses anything under 10). Two factors crossed: the tool fully on (roster on all three layers, groups + coupling + cohesion + ecology + water job + scheduler) against wholly off, under two load levels — the fort as it stands, and the same fort with sixty extra land animals placed at t0 so the unit loop carries roughly three times the work. Placement uses the tool's own headless route in BOTH arms, so the crowd is identical and only the jobs differ. A season, two replicates per arm.
